当前音频系统主要由 src/shared/musicLibrary.js、src/shared/audioScheduling.js、src/shared/boardAudio.js、src/shared/voiceEffects.js 与 src/audio/ 下的播放运行时组成。

  • src/audio/audioSettings.js
    • Owns default audio settings, localStorage loading, per-channel volume calculation, and finite-number sanitization for persisted percent values.
    • Audio settings also support optional per-channel mute flags under muted; audioVolume() returns 0 when muted.master or the requested channel flag is true, while keeping the stored 0-100 slider percentage unchanged.
  • src/audio/backgroundDucking.js
    • Owns the shared voice-active counter and BGM ducking subscribers so voice playback can reduce and restore background-music volume without embedding that state in playback.jsx.
  • src/audio/audioRuntime.js
    • Owns browser Web Audio constructor detection so playback submodules can no-op safely outside browser audio environments.
  • src/audio/proceduralSounds.js
    • Owns generated short SFX such as countdown beeps and doorbell tones; src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
  • src/audio/backgroundMusic.jsx
    • Owns the React BackgroundMusic component, BGM Web Audio scheduling, intro-to-loop timing, decoded BGM buffer cache, autoplay resume fallback, and reconnect recovery guard. src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
  • src/audio/effectPlayback.js
    • Owns fixed SFX asset constants, effect preloading/decoded cache, effect Web Audio playback, HTMLAudio fallback, no-browser no-op protection, and board sound routing for stone/capture/hidden-hand reveal effects. It also owns UI interaction SFX constants for ui_confirm.ogg, ui_close_window.ogg, ui_house_open.ogg, ui_match_open.ogg, ui_detail_open.ogg, ui_shop_open.ogg, and ui_unavailable.ogg. src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
    • src/app/InteractionFeedback.jsx installs document-level interaction feedback: enabled button-like controls play the confirm SFX, disabled/locked controls play the unavailable SFX, and the unavailable target receives a ui-unavailable-shake animation whose duration matches ui_unavailable.ogg. The unavailable animation restarts by removing the class and re-adding it on the next animation frame, avoiding synchronous layout reads on high-frequency disabled taps. Specialized interactions can set data-ui-sound="none" to avoid double-playing the generic confirm cue. Room board interactions, player-info panels, the room title/info stack, and replay control bars are quiet zones for generic confirm SFX so normal moves, high-frequency info taps, and replay stepping do not fire UI prompt sounds; replay disabled buttons can still fire the unavailable SFX.
  • src/audio/playback.jsx
    • Owns voice playback, preloaded voice buffers, TTS, and compatibility re-exports for the split audio modules.
  • BGM 资源配置集中在 MUSIC_TRACKS，按 home、battle、skill 三类管理。
  • 用户音乐库存与选择使用 User.ownedMusicIds 和 User.musicSelections 持久化；公开用户信息会自动并入 defaultUnlocked 曲目。角色技能 BGM 选择保存在 musicSelections.skill[characterId]，对局中技能预览和最近技能角色 BGM 都优先使用该选择，未拥有或角色不匹配时回退默认曲目。
  • 角色详情弹窗右上角包含独立的角色 BGM 预览播放器。播放器显示当前曲名，左侧播放/暂停按钮与播放器胶囊连为一体，支持播放/暂停并保留 intro/loop 进度；当该用户拥有同一角色的多首技能 BGM 时显示下拉选择，切换后通过 /api/me/music-selection 持久化并同步影响后续对局 BGM。
  • Character detail BGM preview enters an immediate loading UI state before first-use WebAudio fetch/decode completes, keeps aria-busy on the local player during startup, and releases the global background-music pause request if startup fails or the selected track changes. Its Rough.js sketch layer is decorative and generated after mount, outside hover/click handlers, so player interaction feedback is not coupled to audio decoding or hand-drawn SVG generation. The Rough.js layer owns the visible player frame and play-button ring; CSS borders remain disabled to prevent duplicate outlines while still providing paper texture and state fills.
  • 角色 BGM 预览播放期间会向全局 BackgroundMusic 发出暂停请求；背景 BGM 会停止当前 source、保存 offset，并在预览暂停、切歌或关闭详情弹窗释放请求后按 offset 重新调度，从原进度恢复，避免双 BGM 叠播或大厅 BGM 从头重启。
  • 主界面默认 BGM 使用已可无缝循环的单文件曲目 main_bgm.ogg。
  • 对弈常规 BGM 使用 shanjifu_intro_once.ogg + shanjifu_loop.ogg。
  • 角色技能 BGM 当前配置：达妮娅使用 canonical denia-skill-default（bgm_*），西格莉卡使用 sigrika_intro_once.ogg + sigrika_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“小爱出击”使用 aemeath0_once.ogg + aemeath0_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“远航星”使用 lhl_intro_once.ogg + lhl_loop.ogg，猪小仙使用 matoya_*，娜波摩使用 busizhe_*，仇远默认使用 qiuyuan_intro_once.ogg + qiuyuan_loop.ogg，仇远第二版可购买技能 BGM qiuyuan-skill-zhouwo 使用 /assets/music/qiuyuan_zhouwo_loop.ogg 单文件循环，商城商品图使用 /assets/items/qiuyuan-zhouwo.webp，启动内置商城 seed 会为已存在且 imageUrl 为空的该音乐商品回填图片且不覆盖非空后台配置，千咲使用 chisa_intro_once.ogg + chisa_loop.ogg，长离使用 changli_intro_once.ogg + changli_loop.ogg，莫宁使用 mornye_intro_once.ogg + mornye_loop.ogg。旧达妮娅 slug 会在启动清理阶段迁移/删除，运行时 BGM resolver 只按 canonical denia 查找达妮娅技能 BGM。
  • BGM 优先级为：角色技能 BGM > 对弈常规 BGM > 主界面 BGM。
  • 角色释放技能后，后续 BGM 保持为该角色专属 BGM，直到对局结束；后触发的角色技能 BGM 会覆盖先触发的角色技能 BGM。
  • 匹配成功倒计时、对局结果弹窗、对局房间的结束阶段会停止 BGM；如果用户从已结束棋谱回放退出到大厅，前端会清空回放房间快照，BGM resolver 以当前 view = home 为准恢复主界面 BGM。在线玩家从已结束房间主动退出回大厅时，前端也会把该房间号记为已处理结果，防止房间 5 分钟回收前的残留终局快照继续触发结果弹窗状态并压住大厅 BGM。
  • 持久登录自动进入大厅时可能没有用户手势，浏览器会继续挂起 AudioContext；BackgroundMusic 会在 resume 后检查上下文状态，如果仍为 suspended，就注册 pointerdown / keydown / touchstart 一次性重试，保证首次点击、按键或触摸后恢复大厅 BGM。
  • BackgroundMusic 使用 Web Audio 播放，预解码 intro/loop buffer，并在同一 AudioContext 时间线上调度 intro 到 loop，避免 HTMLAudio 切文件造成明显卡顿。
  • 音量变化只调节 gain，不改变播放 identity；因此主音量或 BGM 音量调到 0 后再恢复，不会导致 BGM 从头播放。
  • 默认音频设置为 master: 80、bgm: 50、sfx: 80、voice: 80，保存于 localStorage 的 sigrika-audio-settings；运行时音量计算会把非法或非有限百分比值回退到默认值，避免损坏的本地设置把 NaN 传入 Audio/Web Audio。
  • audioRuntime.js 通过统一 helper 暴露浏览器 AudioContext 构造器；倒计时 beep、门铃音和 TTS 在缺少 window、AudioContext 或 speechSynthesis 时静默 no-op，避免测试、预渲染或非浏览器执行路径因音频副作用抛错。
  • 匹配成功后先播放原有 3 秒匹配成功倒计时弹窗，倒计时结束后才进入对局资源预加载页并停止 BGM；该页复用登录加载页样式，但不显示转圈动画，只显示本机玩家出战角色跳动立绘、该角色的加载台词，以及 资源加载中 0/2、1/2 或 2/2，客户端完成本地资源预载后发出 room:preload-ready。进入对局页需要同时满足匹配成功倒计时完成和双方都 ready：倒计时先结束时进入并继续停在资源加载页，资源先 ready 时继续显示倒计时并在倒计时结束后直接进入对局页；服务端 90 秒未等齐会中止匹配并由前端 toast 一方加载超时，匹配中止。
  • 对局结果弹窗出现时停止 BGM；胜方播放 result-victory.mp3，负方播放 result-defeat.mp3，和棋不播放结果音效。房间玩家还会按当前用户本局结果触发出战角色的 result-victory、result-defeat 或 result-draw 系统语音；无效局不弹结果弹窗，因此不触发结果音效或结果角色语音。棋谱回放模式不会弹结果弹窗，也不会触发结果音效或结果角色语音。
  • 普通落子播放 godown_clear.ogg。
  • 提子动作播放 go_capture_clear.ogg。
  • 隐藏手暴露播放 hidden_hand_reveal.ogg；回放下一步遇到带有隐藏手暴露标记的历史动作时也会播放该音效。
  • boardAudio.js 根据历史记录中的当前棋盘动作判断落子音或提子音；回放界面点击“下一手”时也会按该步历史播放对应音效，弃手不会重复播放上一手棋盘音效。
  • 落子、提子、隐藏手暴露、匹配成功和结果音效均走 sfx 音量通道。
  • ??????? ui_confirm.ogg???????? ui_close_window.ogg?????????? ui_house_open.ogg?????????? ui_match_open.ogg???????????? ui_detail_open.ogg???????? ui_shop_open.ogg???????? recruitment-open.ogg???????? recruitment-success.ogg / recruitment-miss.ogg????????? ui_unavailable.ogg ?? sfx ?????.close-button / .inline-close ? data-ui-sound="close" ???????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????/??????????? ui-unavailable-shake ???????????????????????/???????????????? UI ??????????????????????????
  • 星炬棋盘技能特效的程序化音效由 src/room/boardSkillEffectSoundScheduler.js 按 catalog cue 点调度，并由 src/audio/skillEffectSounds.js 负责真正发声；这些音效只读 audioSettings.sfx，不参与服务端结算，也不会在 reduced-motion 棋盘演出中播放爆炸/飞入类音效。src/shared/skillPresentation.js 会先判断该技能演出和音效层是否启用，关闭特效时不进入 SFX 调度；后台 skillEffectsEnabled 关闭后，服务端 pending skill preview 也只等待技能横幅时长，随后直接广播技能结算结果。src/room/pixiPrewarm.js 在技能可用棋盘空闲时预热 pixi.js，真实演出时复用同一个加载 promise，避免首个技能把模块加载成本集中到动画开始时；关闭特效或无技能棋盘不会为技能演出预热 Pixi。
  • row-slash 使用 skillEffectSoundCues cue 元数据调度程序化技能音效；技能横幅结束后通过 BoardSkillEffects 预热并播放不依赖图片资源的 Pixi Graphics 水墨刀光，结算后的持久棋盘刀痕仍走 DOM/CSS overlay，没有独立刀光图片或音频文件依赖。protocol-takeover 使用程序化淡紫与冰蓝数据流 Pixi 光束演出，并通过 WebAudio 程序化播放更明显的两段式数据束启动与目标锁定 SFX，结算后的协议禁入标记继续走 DOM/CSS overlay。double-move 使用程序化火焰 SFX，并把凤凰与火焰透明 SVG 加入 RUNTIME_IMAGE_ASSETS.effects 延迟预加载，技能横幅后的全盘演出通过 Pixi 复用纹理，实际连落点位继续通过 DOM/CSS 显示持续火焰。
  • 娜波摩被动灰白化层由 BoardAmbientEffects 根据 game.passives.*.colorIllusion.active 派生，使用 pointer-transparent 的 CSS 中心向外扩散波纹表现发动过程，同时 .board-wrap.color-illusion-board-surface::before 会把棋盘背景从中心扩散切换到 /assets/boards/nabomo-color-illusion-board.webp；该专用 WebP 由 C:/codex/image/go-board-background-reference-color-vertical-2048.jpg 转换而来，并作为效果资源延迟预加载。该被动不再使用持续 Pixi 烟雾或每帧 ticker 绘制，也不允许回退为整块矩形半透明贴层，因此不会影响落子、触控确认、数子标记或观战/回放棋盘读取。
  • 读秒 10 到 1 秒倒计时通过 playSystemVoice(countdown-N) 播放角色语音或 TTS；超时只作为结算状态，不播放系统音频、角色语音或“还剩0次读秒”。
  • 角色技能语音配置集中在 CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES，派生技能等效果特定语音通过 SYSTEM_VOICE_SKILL_EVENTS 的模式化事件覆盖普通 skill-cast。
  • CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES 支持单个路径或候选路径数组；resolveSystemVoice 每次解析数组候选时随机选择一个，loginPreloadAssets() 与 battlePreloadAssets() 会展开候选数组，保证所有可能播放的语音都纳入预加载。
  • 当前已配置：西格莉卡技能发动使用 sigrika_skill_cast.ogg，爱弥斯普通技能使用 aemeath_skill_cast.ogg，爱弥斯“远航星”通过 skill-cast:voyage-star 使用 aemeath_skill_cast_voyage.ogg，娜波摩使用 nabomo_skill_cast.ogg，猪小仙使用 baconbits_skill_cast.ogg，琳奈使用 lynae_skill_cast.ogg，长离使用 changli_skill_cast.ogg，千咲使用 chisa_skill_cast.ogg，莫宁使用 mornye_skill_cast.ogg。
  • 仇远技能发动语音使用候选 qiuyuan_skill_cast.ogg 与 qiuyuan_skill_cast_1.ogg，每次释放技能随机播放其中一个。
  • characterVoiceMapForSkill 可将现有技能语音桥接为角色 systemVoices 的 skill-cast 事件映射。
  • 角色出战按钮会触发 sortie 角色语音事件；当前达妮娅、西格莉卡、猪小仙、琳奈、长离、千咲和莫宁已分别接入 denia_sortie.ogg、sigrika_sortie.ogg、baconbits_sortie.ogg、lynae_sortie.ogg、changli_sortie.ogg、chisa_sortie.ogg 与 mornye_sortie.ogg。
  • SkillBanner 出现时同步触发技能语音，同一个 banner id 只播放一次。
  • 技能语音走 voice 音量通道。
  • playVoiceSound 使用 Web Audio 播放链：source -> RMS normalization gain -> dry/wet reverb mix -> voice gain -> destination。
  • 同一客户端只允许一个角色语音处于活动播放状态；新的角色语音或 TTS 开始前会立即停止上一段角色语音，避免同一用户连续触发角色语音时叠音。
  • 莫宁（mornye）已接入 game-start、sortie、skill-cast、byo-yomi-start、byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1、countdown-10 到 countdown-1、result-victory、result-defeat、result-draw 与 house-detail 语音；house-detail 使用 /assets/voice/mornye_detail.ogg，打开角色详情或点击详情内“描述”文本区域都会播放该语音，关闭角色详情弹窗或关闭整个部员手册会调用 stopVoicePlayback() 停止当前角色语音。
  • 语音播放开始时会通知 BGM ducking 状态，背景音乐临时压到基础 BGM 音量的 35%；语音结束后约 180ms 平滑恢复，避免角色语音被角色 BGM 盖住。
  • voiceEffects.js 当前预设为轻量空灵混响：boost: 1.35、wet: 0.28、dry: 0.9、reverbSeconds: 1.6、reverbDecay: 2.2、preDelaySeconds: 0.035，并以 targetRms: 0.12、minNormalizationGain: 0.4、maxNormalizationGain: 2.4 做运行时语音响度一致化。
  • 如果 Web Audio 或资源加载失败，技能语音会回退到普通 Audio 播放，仍应用 1.35 倍 voice 增益上限。
  • 系统语音事件集中在 src/shared/systemVoices.js，通过 resolveSystemVoice 解析。
  • 当前默认走 TTS 文本；如果角色配置了 systemVoices[event]，则优先播放对应音频。
  • 已预留事件：game-start、game-start:gomoku、skill-cast、sortie、byo-yomi-start、byo-yomi-periods、byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1、byo-yomi-countdown、countdown-N、timeout、result-victory、result-defeat、result-draw、house-detail。
  • 对局正式开始时，服务端写入 kind 为 game-start 的系统消息，前端据此播放“对局开始”语音。
  • game-start:gomoku 是 game-start 的模式化角色语音变体；当前长离在五子棋模式开局时优先播放 changli_wuzi_match_start.ogg，其它模式继续播放 changli_match_start.ogg；长离倒计时已覆盖 10 到 1 秒。
  • 进入读秒和剩余读秒次数已经接入事件化 resolver；剩余 2/1 次读秒使用显式 byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1 事件，10 到 1 秒倒计时使用 countdown-10 到 countdown-1 角色语音事件。timeout 不再解析为角色音频或 TTS 文本。
  • BGM 和效果音位于 public/assets/music/。
  • 技能语音位于 public/assets/voice/。
  • 转码用临时工具目录 .tmp/ 已加入 .gitignore，不应提交。
  • Runtime audio playback now lives across focused src/audio/ modules: audio settings, BGM ducking, browser audio runtime detection, procedural generated sounds, background music scheduling, and effect playback are split out, while playback.jsx keeps voice/TTS helpers plus compatibility re-exports. Future cleanup can continue splitting voice and audio-cache concerns if the feature set grows.
  • Voice playback uses a shared module-level Web Audio context when available; regular voice playback, cached playback, and preload decoding all reuse that context. Future tuning may still need more explicit lifecycle cleanup and browser autoplay handling.
  • Audio settings, background music ducking, runtime voice loudness helpers, and no-browser audio API guards are covered by deterministic helper tests, but not by a browser-level audio graph test.
  • Runtime voice loudness normalization uses decoded AudioBuffer RMS statistics and clamps correction gain between 0.4 and 2.4, so authored assets can remain unchanged while character voices play at a closer perceived level.
  • Voice reverb now uses a deterministic generated impulse. If authored reverb tails become important, replace it with a static impulse asset.
  • BGM assets are committed directly under public/assets/music/ and increase repository size. If the soundtrack grows, consider Git LFS, an asset CDN, or a manifest-driven asset pipeline.
  • Music ownership, purchase availability, and player selection are represented in configuration shape but not yet backed by persisted music inventory/settings UI.
  • Character skill voice configuration is static in CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES; future admin-configurable voices would need schema/API/upload support and cache invalidation rules.
  • No browser-level automated test currently verifies actual audio playback, Web Audio graph routing, or autoplay fallback behavior; existing coverage focuses on deterministic helper logic.
  • Countdown character voice assets are opportunistically preloaded and decoded into a module-level Web Audio buffer cache while the active player is in byo-yomi. Cached playback reuses the same dry/wet reverb voice chain as regular voice playback, and failures fall back to the existing voice path without blocking timers.

Music Display Name Management

  • Current effective preload contract: login preload blocks home entry on current-user-accessible resources rather than on every configured backend resource. It includes visible home assets, all shop product display images, owned inventory/recruitment item images, visible/owned character detail assets, equipped achievement assets, owned/default BGM that the user can actually reach, and relevant character/system voices. Unpurchased music products do not preload full BGM audio because the player cannot preview them. Battle preload is scoped to the current room, players, and mode; no-skill modes such as standard and gomoku skip skill BGM, skill voices, and skill effect images. A refreshed or reconnected player that resumes a room still in GAME_PHASES.preloading must return to the battle preload route and emit room:preload-ready after local assets finish, instead of entering active room state from that snapshot; this report is retried until the server acknowledges it.
  • Current effective cache contract: production gives only hashed Vite build outputs Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable. Public runtime resources under /assets/**, including skill WebP/SVG, BGM, voice, and theme images, keep the shorter Express static cache with ETag validation so cloud deployments can replace same-name runtime assets without clients being pinned to stale immutable copies. Hash or version filenames remain recommended for high-traffic resource replacements, but the server no longer assumes every /assets/** file is immutable.
  • Local stability verification uses LOCAL_PROD_STATIC=1 to exercise this same cache contract against the production build without setting NODE_ENV=production. This keeps local socket/auth/test-action behavior usable while still catching stale-cache regressions before a cloud deploy. npm run verify:stability now also covers desktop/mobile room recovery, real Pixi .board-effects-canvas playback for Sigrika and Aemeath hidden-hand / voyage-star, finished-room exit plus reload without reopening the result modal, and opponent reconnect without showing the local player a rate-limit toast.
  • Startup preload may show the generic login preloading route only while no room or pending match has already been restored by Socket.IO. If roomRef.current or matchSuccessRef.current exists after /api/me, startup asset loading continues without hiding the recovered battle route.
  • MUSIC_TRACKS remains the authority for playable BGM ids, types, character bindings, default unlock flags, purchasability, playback modes, and audio source paths.
  • MusicTrackSetting stores only per-track displayName overrides keyed by static track id. Blank or missing settings fall back to the static track.name.
  • Server startup runs ensureMusicTrackSettingsSchema() so older SQLite development databases can read and write music display-name settings without a manual migration.
  • Authenticated players read the merged catalog from GET /api/music-tracks; admin users read and update the same catalog through GET /api/admin/music-tracks and PATCH /api/admin/music-tracks/:id.
  • The admin music tab edits display names only. It cannot create tracks, upload or replace audio files, disable tracks, or change track type/character binding.
  • Frontend music consumers should pass the merged track map into existing music-library resolver functions instead of duplicating selection logic. Character BGM preview/select controls, background music resolution, shop music card names, and gacha music prize options/display names should all use the merged catalog.

Login Asset Preloading

  • Frontend deployment helpers live in src/shared/preloadAssets.js.
  • Socket.IO now connects to window.location.origin, so the deployed site can run behind https://sigrika.fun without a hard-coded localhost socket endpoint.
  • Vite development proxy forwards /socket.io websocket traffic to the local backend, keeping the same-origin socket path usable in development and production.
  • After a valid token is confirmed, the app enters a preloading view before the home screen.
  • Fresh login enters preloading before the home screen, preventing the home screen from flashing before assets begin loading. Stored-token startup is intentionally disabled so refresh and browser restart return to the login screen.
  • The preload step fetches non-replay runtime assets after login and blocks home entry on the current user's accessible asset manifest. That manifest includes current-user-visible character portraits, home entry/background imagery, shop product display images, owned inventory/recruitment item imagery, equipped achievement asset images, owned/default-unlocked BGM sources, relevant owned/default character skill BGM, character skill voices, system voices, and common board/UI effect sounds. Unpurchased music products only contribute shop display imagery, not full BGM audio, because the player cannot preview unowned music. Replay lists and replay details remain lazy data requests so opening the app does not prefetch historical game records.

Aemeath Voyage Star Resources

  • voyage-star is a full-board Pixi skill effect backed by /assets/effects/voyage-star-crater.webp, a transparent WebP converted from the supplied heart/star artwork. src/room/boardSkillEffectRegistry.js draws the white/ice-blue knight greatsword, target omen, impact glow, visual-only quake layer, yellow-white soft-edged explosion cover, and removal particles from pending preview geometry while leaving the crater artwork to the resolved board overlay. The greatsword drops from above with a short trail and stays visible through impact; the impact window adds concentric earthquake ellipses, 2-3 decaying shake pulses on the Pixi layer only, and deterministic dust sparks without moving the DOM board or hit areas. The explosion cover layer is added after the other Pixi layers so it can fully mask the impact frame; it expands from the skill center with a solid warm-white core whose radius is at least 2.2 board cells, covering the roughly four-cell crater footprint, then blends outward through low-alpha white and warm-yellow discs whose feathered outer edge fades to zero alpha. The separate impact glow fades before full-cover, then the renderer adds a fully opaque white full-board cover with no circular rim, holds that cover for resolution, and dissolves it with eased opacity plus outward yellow-white rings, radial streaks, and spark fragments instead of snapping directly back to the board; the sword alpha is forced to zero once that cover reaches every board corner. The Pixi cast starts from procedural Graphics and never draws a temporary crater sprite or fallback shape; the crater WebP is loaded through the renderer asset list only as monitored prewarm for the resolved DOM crater, so slow image decode cannot delay the real Pixi canvas or the full-board cast startup. skillPreviewResolutionDelay({ effectType: "voyage-star" }) resolves at 2936ms, which is the 2s banner plus 52% of the 1800ms board-effect window, placing the authoritative crater and stone removals under the full-cover explosion light. BoardSkillEffects keeps an already-started Pixi cast alive after that early resolved snapshot clears pendingSkill, so the opaque cover hides the resolved board until its dissolve phase instead of letting the crater show through the whiteout. The persistent DOM crater is a board-level .voyage-star-crater-mark overlay positioned by board-coordinate CSS variables from the source id, sized to 3.5 board cells, and animated with tight pink-purple/cyan drop-shadow glow that follows the WebP alpha shape instead of a round haze; the erased points remain stored on the points as voyage-star-crater-point or voyage-star-erased-point. This one-owner split keeps the crater from appearing inside the cover, avoids a sudden post-effect crater pop, and avoids a second flash when the resolved room state arrives.
  • aemeath-skill-default is the selectable base Aemeath “小爱出击” BGM and uses /assets/music/aemeath0_once.ogg followed by /assets/music/aemeath0_loop.ogg. aemeath-voyage-star-default is also selectable in Aemeath's regular character BGM list, but Voyage Star pending skill previews still provide musicTrackId: "aemeath-voyage-star-default", so findSkillTrack() forces the previous /assets/music/lhl_intro_once.ogg + /assets/music/lhl_loop.ogg track for the derived skill regardless of the player's current Aemeath BGM selection. After pendingSkill clears, background music resolution derives latestSkillPreview() from room history and preserves effectType plus musicTrackId, so Voyage Star does not fall back to the base Aemeath skill BGM during its resolved animation window. battlePreloadAssets() preloads derived skill tracks by effectType, so Voyage Star remains preloaded even though the track is selectable. Future purchasable Aemeath skill BGMs should use the existing character-level selection only when they replace the base “小爱出击” default; if a shop track is meant to replace a derived skill such as Voyage Star, the selection model should add an effect-scoped key such as aemeath:voyage-star (or an equivalent nested shape) and resolve that scoped choice before falling back to musicSelections.skill.aemeath.
  • The Voyage Star track intentionally keeps Aemeath's previous lhl_* intro/loop files, while the base Aemeath track now uses the supplied aemeath0_* assets.
  • Preload failures are non-blocking per resource: failed or hanging asset loaders are skipped after a bounded per-task timeout so users are not trapped on the loading screen if a single asset stalls during reconnect, server restart, or cache recovery. Skipped login and battle assets are retried in the background after the target screen is entered with a lower concurrency cap.
  • The preload screen uses a character portrait hop plus progress bar, with a short minimum display duration to avoid a visual flash on cached loads. AssetPreloadScreen reads siteSettings.characterLoadingLines as characterId=line rows for the main loading line, uses a random catalog character for post-login preload, and uses the current player character for battle preload; the spinner remains only as an image-missing fallback. It also reads siteSettings.preloadTips, parses one non-empty tip per line, shows one random tip below the progress bar, and on the post-login preload view rotates the random character portrait, character loading line, and tip together every 10 seconds while the view remains open; fixed-character battle preload keeps the player character stable and only rotates tips. The loading panel itself stays borderless and transparent in both the base layer and the final Bright School safety layer, uses full-viewport centering, and lets the character line plus tip text wrap inside the shared preload width so mobile and desktop keep the same no-frame loading treatment without adding a solid middle panel background.

---

UI 主题、移动端与交互体验

本文记录 Bright School 主题、CSS 分层、移动端适配、弹窗布局、反馈消息和 UI 交互体验。新增主题、移动布局、弹窗视觉合同或用户反馈界面时优先更新本分篇。

当前结论

  • 玩家侧目前只有 Bright School 一个默认主题，但主题注册、作用域和 CSS 入口仍保留扩展口。
  • src/styles/base.css 现在是 import-only 共享基础入口，具体规则拆到 src/styles/base/ 下的 foundation、asset-preload、surfaces-forms-actions、topbar-room-tags、home-legacy-grid、home-stage-artboard、home-stage-toolbox 和 message-feedback；src/styles/room.css 现在是 import-only 房间入口，具体规则拆到 src/styles/room/ 下的 layout-tabs、players-timers-skills、import-only board、actions-requests、people-floating-replay 和 chat-responsive，其中 room/board.css 再转入 room/board/ 下的 frame/coordinates、row slash、ambient fog、effect canvas/motion、points/preview、stones/skill effects、latest/touch/void 和 grid/scoring 分篇；src/styles/modals.css 是 import-only 共享弹窗入口，具体规则拆到 src/styles/modals/ 下的 base-result-skill、import-only replay-mode-resume、nested-profile、profile-character-records、profile-hero-cleanup、profile-social-actions、character-opening、phone 和 terminal-system，其中 modals/replay-mode-resume.css 再转入 modals/replay-mode-resume/ 下的 replay list/table、resume header actions、match mode/tabs、resume layout、achievement modal、personalization preview/grid、personalization picker 和 resume character records 分篇；src/styles/commerce-settings.css 也是 import-only 领域入口，具体规则拆到 src/styles/commerce/gacha.css、social-profile.css、shop-settings.css、terminal-polish.css、warehouse-toast.css；src/styles/responsive.css 作为共享响应式入口，按 900px 平板/窄桌面、900px 横屏、760px 紧凑布局、760px 竖屏房间、短横屏房间和 620px 小屏表单/棋盘转入 src/styles/responsive/；src/styles/mobile-room.css 作为共享移动对局入口，按基础 shell/dock、竖屏房间、超窄竖屏、横屏房间、短横屏房间和 reduced-motion 转入 src/styles/mobile-room/；src/styles/hud-components.css 作为共享 HUD 兼容入口，按 HUD 硬化、窄屏 HUD 调整、pop-tech 终端风格、成员手册可读性、角色连锁徽章和用户身份装饰转入 src/styles/hud-components/；src/styles/mobile-adaptive.css   • 移动端布局由共享移动层、Bright School 移动层和最终 mobile-adaptive.css 安全层共同约束。设置弹窗在最终手机层使用标题、tab、内部面板三行网格，外壳不滚动，当前 settings-panel 自己滚动并 containment；关闭按钮、首页图标按钮和手机菜单按钮保持显式 accessible label 与 44px 触控目标。

Mobile touch confirmation must render only the inner .touch-confirm-marker; .point.touch-confirming::before stays hidden, and confirmation feedback may brighten the point but must not scale the whole point button.
  • Tailwind CSS is available only as a low-intrusion utility layer through src/styles/tailwind.css, imported from src/styles.css after hud-components.css and immediately before src/styles/themes.css. It uses the Tailwind v4 Vite plugin, imports tailwindcss/theme.css and tailwindcss/utilities.css with prefix(tw), and deliberately omits preflight so Bright School, board, modal, and mobile safety layers keep their existing element and component contracts. New Tailwind usage should use tw: classes and should not replace existing theme-owned CSS surfaces without a focused migration plan; this cleanup goal does not introduce a component library or migrate current room, skill, board, or final-mobile CSS to utilities. Future player themes remain CSS-entry based through themes.css until imported, scoped, and visually verified.

Modal Layering

  • Shared .modal-backdrop stacks above room --room-floating-z surfaces so request and confirmation modals dim skill chips, chat controls, and room member popovers together.
  • Player story playback uses a dedicated top modal layer on .onboarding-story-backdrop, above ordinary .modal-backdrop sheets and the final mobile modal safeguard, so item-triggered stories remain clickable while the warehouse or other business modal stays underneath. Story dismissal is centralized: opening the generic player clears the legacy onboarding-story flag, and closing/skipping/back dismissal clears both story overlay flags plus the active script. Admin embedded story previews reset that backdrop to normal document stacking with z-index: auto.
  • Mobile room portraits do not render character-chain badges; duplicate-chain data remains in user payloads but is hidden on player-facing portrait surfaces.

Feedback Messages

  • Player feedback is persisted in the FeedbackMessage Prisma model, backed by the 202605250001_add_feedback_message migration.
  • Logged-in users submit feedback through POST /api/feedback; the server stores userId, current username, normalized content, and createdAt.
  • Feedback content strips control characters, trims whitespace, rejects empty submissions, and caps content at 400 characters.
  • The home and room message-board modal calls the feedback API. On success it closes the modal and shows a green top toast: 感谢您的反馈！.
  • The message-board textarea uses the same 400-character limit and displays the remaining character count below the input.
  • Admins can view the latest 100 feedback messages through GET /api/admin/feedback.
  • The admin console includes a 留言反馈 tab that displays submit time, username, and full feedback content with wrapping for longer text.

Mobile Responsive Adaptation

  • src/styles/mobile-adaptive.css is imported at the end of src/styles/themes.css, preserving the existing styles.css theme-layer contract while making this the final 320px-768px mobile safety layer. The entry is import-only and delegates concrete rules to src/styles/mobile-adaptive/, while keeping existing visual themes and component markup intact and enforcing no page-level horizontal overflow, 44px minimum touch targets for buttons/tabs/icon controls, 16px mobile form inputs, safe-area padding, and media elements constrained to their containers. The portrait mobile battle-room sub-entry src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait.css is nested import-only and delegates shell/header/menu, neutral chrome reset, viewport shell, player strips, board viewport, dock panels, and action/decision controls to src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait/. The announcement detail sub-entry src/styles/mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail.css is also nested import-only and exists specifically to keep the embedded detail layer the same size as its parent announcement sheet after generic nested-modal and Bright School mobile overrides.
  • The shared commerce sub-entry src/styles/commerce/shop-settings.css is import-only and delegates shop shell/tabs, settings panel, shop cards, detail/pagination, compact shop media, compact modal safety, and phone layouts to src/styles/commerce/shop-settings/. This keeps shop, settings, leaderboard/friends mobile guards, and warehouse/house phone fixes in named files while preserving the existing commerce-settings.css import position. Shop card purchase actions must keep unavailable states as native disabled controls and render sold out, already owned, not purchasable, pending purchase, and insufficient-coin buttons with one gray inactive treatment across base, Bright School, and final mobile layers.
  • The shared gacha modal sub-entry src/styles/commerce/gacha.css is also import-only and delegates modal tabs, featured stack, machine stage, featured prize, control panel, list/result dialogs, and animations to src/styles/commerce/gacha/, preserving the existing commerce layer order while keeping result grid and animation rules in named files.
  • The shared recruitment modal sub-entry follows the same import-only commerce style contract through src/styles/commerce/recruitment.css and src/styles/commerce/recruitment/, with mobile safety rules in src/styles/mobile-adaptive/phone-recruitment.css. The modal is intentionally arranged as three stable rows: header information, a bulletin-board main panel for selected/pending/ready/result states, and one-line action controls for recruitment item selection and confirmation on both desktop and mobile. The bulletin-board main panel uses /assets/recruitment/notice-board-flat-candidate.webp, and Bright School recruitment overrides must preserve --recruitment-board-background-image instead of replacing the second row with a flat color. The image uses proportional cover sizing with centered positioning so the second row crops from the vertical center instead of stretching the artwork. Ready/no-response and success states may layer the flat envelope and celebration sticker assets, but modal text and controls stay DOM/CSS-owned for readability and responsive safety. The compact header uses the single title 部员招募栏; pending recruitment hides the helper label and renders only the remaining time digits as CRT-style green glowing tabular text without a screen background or border; mobile item selection can drop item names and keep only icons plus quantities when the row is too narrow, selected-item confidence copy uses a red secondary cue, and unavailable recruitment confirmation must stay a gray native disabled button in base, mobile, and Bright School layers.
  • The shared social/profile/leaderboard sub-entry src/styles/commerce/social-profile.css is also import-only and delegates modal shells, friends toolbar/search, friends list/status, friend actions/notices, duel request banner, leaderboard table, and owned decoration section rules to src/styles/commerce/social-profile/, preserving the existing commerce layer order while keeping social and leaderboard contracts in named files.
  • General CSS architecture guard coverage lives in src/styles/styleContract.test.js: any CSS file with @import must stay import-only, and new or expanded 6000-byte CSS files fail the contract unless the known-debt baseline is intentionally updated during a split plan.
  • Full-repo CSS cleanup completion gates live in CSS_FULL_REPO_CLEANUP_VERIFICATION_GATES: every CSS stage runs the static CSS contracts, room/board/skill/mobile or broad CSS changes run npm run verify:battle-fixes, protected Pixi/skill-presentation changes run npm run verify:stability -- tests/stability/skill-effects.spec.js, and final handoff runs npm run check.
  • The Startorch commerce/social polish sub-entry src/styles/commerce/terminal-polish.css is also import-only and delegates terminal headers/profile stats, character cards, shop shell/tabs, item cards/empty states, list/status rows, settings/warehouse target surfaces, and compact phone fixes to src/styles/commerce/terminal-polish/, preserving the existing commerce layer order.
  • src/styles/mobile-modals.css is now an import-only mobile modal safety entry after room-terminal.css and before HUD compatibility styles. It delegates backdrop/base mobile constraints, 760px compact modal shells, 560px watch/replay/shop/leaderboard/friends/house-resume phone layouts, and reduced-motion safety to src/styles/mobile-modals/, while modal feature tests read the import tree so the split does not weaken the existing layout contracts.
  • Bright School's final mobile guard entry src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides.css is also import-only. Its concrete rules are split under src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/ by home/auth header, replay dialog, profile/house records, shop cards, leaderboard cards, and deployed-character state, preserving the original 760px portrait cascade order without keeping one large late override file. The profile/house records sub-entry src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records.css is nested import-only as well, delegating house/profile stat fixes, profile resume stats, character record dialog rules, and embedded resume character records to src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records/.
  • The Startorch live-room terminal skin keeps src/styles/room-terminal.css as the root import point after mobile-room.css, but concrete rules now live under src/styles/room-terminal/ for shell/theme variables, header tags, player/timer/skill panels, board/actions, shared panels/chat/replay, portrait mobile, and landscape mobile. Room tests read the import tree so the skin contract follows the split files instead of depending on one monolithic CSS source.
  • Shared room player/timer/skill styling is also nested import-only: src/styles/room/players-timers-skills.css delegates side layout, player cards, capture tooltips, timers, skill chips, mobile tap tooltips, and color badges to src/styles/room/players-timers-skills/.
  • The mobile room layout remains driven by MobileRoomLayout, but portrait and landscape breakpoints now prioritize the board as the primary region. Portrait uses a single-column stack with compact opponent/self player strips, a board size calculated from remaining 100dvh space, and a bounded bottom tab dock. Landscape uses a three-column board-centered grid with player strips as side rails and the dock below, so the board stays visually dominant on phones and narrow tablets. Short landscape player rails hide portrait art, rank/rating tags, capture counters, and skill chips, keeping only the player identity and timer inside the side cards instead of clipping overflow content.
  • MobileRoomLayout is now a shell only. RoomBattleStage owns the rendered mobile battle grid, player strips, board slot, and bottom tab dock, so the layout no longer tries to extract composite React children before they render. The mobile action dock keeps player actions in one five-button touch row with 44px-plus targets, short visible text labels, and a bounded operation-hint region, while low-frequency members/chat panels stay behind tabs. Landscape phone layout keeps a larger board-centered grid instead of compressing the board into a miniature preview; in this mode the action tab suppresses the operation hint, uses a 44px no-padding single-row dock, and shifts the dock upward into the board gap so the five game-action buttons stay inside the short viewport.
  • Modal and sheet behavior is normalized for mobile: shop, house, warehouse, leaderboard, friends, settings, message-board, profile, result, replay, and detail dialogs fit within 100dvh, keep their outer frame inside the viewport, and assign scrolling to internal content regions. Leaderboard, friend rows, watch rows, and replay tables use mobile cards where compact reading matters; only views that truly need table comparison keep local horizontal scrolling instead of causing page overflow. The final mobile-adaptive.css safety layer explicitly keeps friend and watch lists on card-width rows with overflow-x: hidden, so later theme repairs cannot accidentally restore the old wide-table scroll behavior.
  • Mobile interaction feel is owned by the final mobile CSS layers instead of JSX-specific handlers. src/styles/mobile-adaptive.css defines shared tap tokens, removes mobile tap highlights, gives buttons/cards/rows a short 120ms press response, keeps internal scroll containers smooth and contained, and adds lightweight backdrop/sheet entrance animations with a prefers-reduced-motion fallback. Theme and HUD motion should animate transform, opacity, color, or shadow only; progress/timer bars must not declare transition: width, and spring/bounce cubic-bezier curves are avoided in favor of smooth deceleration.
  • Bright School's portrait mobile layer is an import-only entry that mirrors the same interaction contract with high-specificity theme rules: home entries, utility buttons, modal controls, shop/warehouse/house cards, table rows, and room dock tabs all get touch-friendly press feedback; board points keep touch-action: none so taps place/confirm stones without page drag, while previewable and touch-confirming points get a subtle scale/brightness response. Board point buttons are exempt from ordinary button chrome in both shared board CSS and the Bright School refinement-board layer: they stay transparent with no appearance, border, shadow, background image, or inherited 44px min-size, so theme button rules cannot paint a white button sheet above the SVG grid. The .board-lines SVG grid is likewise protected from broad media resets by full-size, no max-size, visible-stroke guard rules.
  • Bright School star points must render their black dot through .point.star::after, not .point::before; .point::before is the shared move-hover and skill-target preview layer, so desktop star-point hover must still show the ordinary move hint.
  • Default black/white stone styles must not use border or inset shadows to form an outline ring; Bright School default stone overrides must stay ringless. Semantic skill states such as hidden-hand, flipped, and double-move may still use glow rings or state halos.
  • Player theme extension is registry-driven. src/app/visualTheme.js owns VISUAL_THEME_OPTIONS for the Settings > Interface list, including disabled future options, and VISUAL_THEMES / VISUAL_THEME_IDS for only enabled themes. Future ids such as club-standard and motari-luxury can appear as disabled settings choices, but sanitizeVisualTheme() still resolves them to bright-school until their CSS entries are imported, scoped, and visually verified.
  • CSS theme refactors must preserve the current cascade boundary: src/styles/themes.css imports shared theme contracts, Bright School, then the final mobile-adaptive.css safety layer. Board and skill presentation selectors are protected during style cleanup: .board .point, .board-lines, .board-effects-canvas, .board-wrap skill state classes, and src/styles/themes/bright-school/effects/ must not be normalized with broad button/media/pseudo-element cleanup because character skill timing and Pixi/DOM layering depend on those contracts.
  • Round-2 CSS cleanup planning is tracked in src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js and enforced by src/styles/cssLayerInventory.test.js. It classifies styles into reorganizable shared domains, high-risk gameplay/room surfaces, Bright School-specific overrides, final mobile safety layers, and skill-presentation protected surfaces. Round-3 low-risk cleanup must stay inside the reorganizable shared domain list unless the work explicitly enters the board/skill visual regression path.
  • Round-3 shared-domain cleanup split base/home-legacy-grid.css, base/home-stage-artboard.css, hud-components/pop-tech-terminal.css, mobile-modals/phone-house-resume.css, modals/character-opening.css, responsive/phone-portrait-room.css, modals/mailbox.css, and commerce/recruitment/board.css into nested import-only directories while preserving their existing cascade positions.   • Gameplay room cleanup uses CSS_GAMEPLAY_ROOM_SPLITS instead of the shared-domain list. room/actions-requests.css now delegates to room/actions-requests/ for room toggles/action bars, decision/scoring panels, timed request toasts, test tools, replay controls, and disabled action states, mobile-room/portrait-room.css delegates to mobile-room/portrait-room/ for shared portrait viewport, player strip, board/dock, decision, and action-hint rules, and room-terminal/players-timers-skills.css delegates to room-terminal/players-timers-skills/ for the Startorch battlefield player panel, timer, captures, skill chip, detail panel, and sheen keyframe skin while preserving their current import positions before later responsive and theme repair layers.

  • Commerce and settings controls use mobile grid tabs/pagination with wrapping labels instead of squeezed inline buttons. Shop mobile layout collapses to a top summary strip plus a two-column item grid, with one-column fallback below 360px; card purchase actions retain full-width 44px touch targets.
  • Bright School's final mobile layer keeps login and home titles on one line by scaling type within bounded widths instead of truncating the main title. The login brand and login/register segmented control no longer add tinted background panels on phones or desktop, so the paper grid remains the dominant surface; the outer login card keeps the same lower-right hard shadow depth as Bright School buttons.
  • Bright School home image entries use the final import-only src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop.css safety layer even on desktop breakpoints. Concrete rules live under src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/ by common region reset, 1181px-1500px wide stage, 1024px-1180px compact stage, 701px-1023px micro-stage scroll, and short-height stack concerns. The member handbook and match entries are image-only buttons: visible data-hud, paper labels, fixed text badges, card bottom label areas, radial glows, and blurred halo shadows are not allowed. Their buttons keep accessible aria-labels, the <img> nodes are decorative and constrained with box-sizing: border-box, height/max-height: 100%, and object-fit: contain, and the final Bright School effects/home-image-entry-buttons.css layer owns non-text hover/focus/active feedback. Desktop hover/focus rotates the entry slightly clockwise and keeps only the single hard image drop-shadow plus focus outline; the later narrow-desktop region reset must preserve that rotation instead of replacing it with lift-only motion. Touch/mobile cannot depend on hover, must keep at least 44px target size, and uses restrained press scaling without layout displacement. prefers-reduced-motion cancels transform movement while preserving focus visibility.
  • Bright School 桌面首页采用四段响应式契约：>=1181px 是 wide desktop，主舞台无横向滚动并让匹配入口成为一级视觉主角；桌面主舞台统一使用两大列，左列从上到下堆叠玩家铭牌、部员手册和 3x2 “社团工具箱”，右列只放跨三行的匹配图片按钮。1181px-1500px 额外压缩列间距、降低舞台顶部留白、放大右侧匹配入口，并给玩家铭牌保留可读列宽，避免 1280x768 等中等桌面裁切段位积分；1024px-1180px 是 compact desktop，使用 player/manual/utility/match 命名网格，不允许页面级横向滚动，并保持左操作栈加右匹配主列；701px-1023px 是 micro desktop，.home-main-panel 提供受控横向滚动，内部 .home-stage 固定 960px 最小舞台宽度，继续保留左操作栈和右匹配入口的层级；<=700px 继续走移动首页布局。核心可点击入口必须在正常 grid flow 内；Bright School 玩家铭牌内部保持头像、用户名、段位面板三列固定结构，用户名只能在中间列内缩放和收敛，不能跨列遮挡段位面板；低高度桌面允许纵向滚动，footer 只在宽桌面且高度充足时固定在视口右下角。桌面工具区每个按钮只包含图标和主标题，移动紧凑规则同样不能隐藏主标题或压缩点击目标。匹配入口下方不展示模式票据；lobbyStats.matchmakingCounts 继续用于点击打开的对弈模式选择卡片，规则文案固定为“路数·时间”与“贴目/规则”两行，计数卡片固定贴到对弈按钮最右侧且“匹配中”保持一行；Bright School 竖屏设置弹窗的标题行高和 tabs 三列布局由最终 portrait 安全层控制，防止标题被裁切或 tab 换行。
  • Bright School portrait home/profile/shop fixes that must survive theme overrides live in the final import-only src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait.css layer and its src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/ domain files. The home player plaque reserves a non-collapsing middle column for UserIdentity, keeps equipped nameplates visible inside the username tag, and keeps character-chain badges hidden so they do not cover character art. The resume header uses a two-row grid on phones: title plus close button on the first row, achievement/personalization shortcuts left on the second row, and the visible coin wallet right on the second row. The final portrait rule repeats the desktop-level resume header selectors so desktop grid-column assignments cannot hide the mobile close button; its wallet slot is shrinkable and keeps the coin capsule inside the modal edge. Embedded resume character records use an internally scrolling list with taller rows instead of compressing avatars and text. Bright School shop wallet display now shows only the coin capsule while blue-gem balances remain hidden from shop and resume surfaces.
  • Bright School home player plaque stats are centered inside the stats panel on desktop and mobile: the stats panel declares an inline-size container, each mode row uses three shrinkable centered columns for mode/rank/rating, and the text uses container query units so ratings stay inside the plaque when the stage or phone width changes.
  • Resume character records in the final Bright School mobile layers mirror the detailed user profile character-record list: the section shell remains lightweight, and each compact row owns avatar/name plus five right-aligned stat columns for total games, wins, losses, draws, and win rate. The embedded resume list and standalone nested record dialogs keep those stats on one line with tabular numbers instead of a single combined record string.
  • Resume and user profile recent-result rows share the same .profile-rank-results visual contract on desktop and mobile: a real .recent-result-label displays "最近十盘的战绩" as small black text above the green/red result chips, rather than as a faint background watermark.
  • Mobile user profile stat cards use centered internal grids for record, rating, and rank. The record card sizes total games and win/loss/draw as two centered lines so long results stay inside the card instead of overflowing.
  • User profile footer actions keep their visual state semantic on desktop and mobile: "对局回放" matches the resume replay button's green treatment, while disabled replay or relationship actions such as "已是好友" or "已在黑名单" use the shared gray inactive treatment instead of the normal action fill.
  • User profile hero cards are left-aligned on desktop and mobile: the portrait stays in the first column, the username/nameplate stays in the second column, and the icon-only like/report controls are absolutely anchored to the hero card's bottom-right corner. The like button may include only the thumbs-up icon plus count, and disabled like/report states must remain native disabled gray controls across base and Bright School layers.
  • Friend-list action rows and room member username popovers keep unavailable social actions as native disabled controls with gray inactive styling across the base, Startorch HUD, and Bright School theme layers; the not-yet-open direct-message entry stays disabled in both surfaces.
  • Mobile leaderboard rows in the final Bright School layer remove extra left list inset, shrink the rank badge column, enlarge character portraits, and center the username/nameplate plus rank stack so the player rank sits directly below the username on the same vertical center axis. Compact leaderboard UserIdentity names also expose a fit font-size token, letting legacy/generated usernames shrink to remain readable before the name would clip. Current-user leaderboard rows, including the pinned "my ranking" row, use a light green surface on desktop and mobile to distinguish player-owned leaderboard information from ordinary entries without changing the section title treatment.
  • Selected tab-like controls and selected option buttons keep one pressed-state language on desktop and mobile. Active, aria-selected, and aria-pressed controls use a shorter shadow, slight downward transform, and inset shadow so the selected state looks physically pressed into the paper surface rather than only color-highlighted.
  • Achievement and personalization modal headers use a shared two-column title/close grid on desktop and mobile. Bright School's final portrait layer repeats the static close-button placement so nested modal close controls stay on the title row instead of drifting onto the divider.
  • Desktop resume modal headers use a four-column title, shortcut, wallet, and close layout. The wallet group and close button both sit in right-aligned static grid columns so currency capsules cannot overlap the close control.
  • Desktop resume modal bodies use a fixed-height internal grid instead of letting the whole modal scroll; the embedded character-record section owns the vertical scrollbar, while .profile-grid.top-stats-bar remains overflow-visible so rating/rank help tooltips are not clipped by the stat region. Desktop user profile detail cards keep the same pattern by bounding .profile-character-list, while character detail headings reserve close-button space so the compact character BGM player and top-right close control do not overlap.
  • Bright School character detail BGM controls use a unified campus notebook treatment on desktop and a separate portrait mobile size contract. The player keeps a fixed hand-drawn Rough.js SVG layer as the only visible outer frame and play-button ring, while CSS supplies lined-paper texture, sticker state fills, and a heavier track-name treatment. Hover/press feedback remains transform-only so the control does not resize or trigger layout shifts. Portrait mobile overrides in both the Bright School mobile layer and final mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait guard keep the player within min(164px, 48vw) and preserve the non-overlap heading grid.
  • Personalization equipment categories use compact style-picker entry buttons instead of listing every decoration inline. The nested picker shows default plus unlocked options, auto-closes on selection, and updates the try-on preview immediately; mobile uses a single-column internal scroll list while desktop uses an adaptive grid.
  • The home footer is rendered from configurable footerText site settings. Desktop treats it as a viewport-fixed HUD sibling after main.home-screen, so it remains in the lower-right browser corner while the lobby stage scrolls; links use the safe Markdown form [label](https://example.com). The mobile home footer remains part of the normal document flow rather than a fixed overlay, keeping copyright/link text below the content stack and preventing it from covering navigation cards or modal content.
  • Nested replay dialogs inside the house modal use a fixed-height grid shell with the replay list as the internal scroll region on phones. Replay rows remain card-like, and long replay histories no longer depend on a bottom horizontal scrollbar or page-level scrolling.
  • House replay cards on narrow phones use a bounded three-row grid with fixed 22px player portraits and single-line player names, so date/result/player/move-count content stays inside each colored row at 375px width.
  • Mobile shop cards now share one compact internal contract across character, item, and decoration categories: preview, name, stock/price, and purchase button stay inside the card with the action pinned to the bottom; disabled purchase buttons remain gray and non-pressing even when Bright School portrait overrides restyle card actions.
  • Key responsive breakpoints are 768px for the general mobile adaptation layer, 768px + portrait for phone/narrow tablet vertical layouts, 768px + landscape for short wide devices, and 360px for the smallest phone fallback. Existing project breakpoints at 900px, 760px, 620px, and 560px remain in place; the final mobile layer only resolves conflicts and enforces mobile usability guarantees.

Achievement And Personalization UI

  • 成就与个性化窗口沿用 Bright School / 纸张 scrapbook 视觉：house-modal 外壳、8px 控件圆角、纸张边框、柔和浅色背景和现有 modal close-button 契约，不引入新的品牌视觉。
  • 移动端 UserIdentity 装饰有 768px 专用保护层：已装备的徽章、称号和用户名背景会同步缩小，用户名背景保持左对齐文字，房间玩家条和成员列表会进一步限制名牌宽度，避免手机端出现横向溢出。
  • UserIdentity nameplates use the shared fixed-ratio badge contract across desktop and mobile: users without a nameplate keep natural-width text, equipped nameplates use a 3.75:1 background slot, and equipped username text uses shared --user-nameplate-font-size: calc(15px * var(--user-nameplate-scale)) so text, background, and safe padding scale together. Scene CSS may change --user-nameplate-scale, but must not define per-surface arbitrary nameplate font sizes or stretch the tag to the parent width. Nameplate PNG assets should still be alpha-trimmed with scripts/pngTrim.mjs and delivered or resampled at 3.75:1.
  • 资料卡头部继续通过 UserIdentity 展示已装备的用户名背景，但资料卡局部会清除身份容器、主行和 name-tag 外层的纯色背板、虚线框、描边和阴影；头像在资料卡内也不加额外边框或阴影。移动端资料卡的点赞/举报按钮可压缩为接近关闭按钮半高的紧凑控件，桌面端保留原按钮尺寸，仅沿用淡蓝点赞和红色举报底色；两端默认态保留按钮阴影，按下态通过轻微下移并去掉阴影表达 pressed depth。资料卡底部的对局回放按钮在桌面和移动端都使用浅绿回放色，禁用回放和不可用关系按钮统一使用灰色渐变、弱化文字、not-allowed 光标；举报输入弹窗使用固定视口居中定位，并在短视口内自己滚动，不能继承房间浮层的右侧锚点；最终 mobile-adaptive/profile-report-dialog.css 安全层会用高优先级宽度和定位覆盖 Bright School 对 .room-floating-modal 的宽面板规则。
  • 成就筛选 tab 使用三等分按钮，桌面端列表保持成就名、内容和奖励三列对齐；unachieved 行使用灰色背景，achieved 行使用浅黄色背景。达成时间通过点击已达成行时从点击位置附近弹出的固定定位时间浮窗展示，不再占用列表列宽，也不参与成就窗口内部排版；浮窗会按桌面或移动视口边界收束。560px 以下隐藏表头并将每条成就变成单列卡片，避免奖励内容挤压。
  • 个性化窗口桌面端使用预览区加三列装备面板，称号、徽章、用户名背景分别独立选择；预览区通过 UserIdentity 展示保存前试穿效果。装备按钮状态遵循成就 tab 的粉红选中视觉：粉红表示当前已保存并生效，浅绿色表示正在试穿但尚未保存。560px 以下改为单列面板，按钮保持 42px 以上高度和内部省略号，保证移动端可点选。
  • 部员手册的出战状态分两层表达：角色卡本体使用浅绿色表示当前出战角色，卡内出战按钮复用 tab/装备按钮的粉红选中视觉并保留 pressed depth；桌面和移动最终层都显式覆盖该状态，避免 disabled 或通用按钮样式把当前出战按钮洗回灰色。
  • toast.achievement 使用金橙色高亮渐变和更强阴影，作为成就达成反馈；普通成功/错误 toast 样式不变。

Mailbox UI And Mobile

  • The home mailbox entry uses a lucide-react mail icon in the existing top-right action cluster on desktop and the compact mobile menu on phones. The badge is the unread plus claimable count from the summary API.
  • src/styles/modals/mailbox.css defines the player mailbox modal as a two-column list/detail layout on desktop and a stacked list/detail layout on mobile, with bounded internal scroll regions so long mail bodies do not push the modal outside the viewport.
  • Empty player inboxes keep the same mailbox layout as populated inboxes: the left mail list renders a compact 暂无邮件 row, and the detail pane stays reserved instead of replacing the whole modal with a separate empty card.
  • The mailbox modal is included in the shared terminal and Bright School modal shell layers so desktop renders as a centered sheet instead of a full-width strip, while mobile keeps a top sheet with safe-area padding.
  • src/styles/admin/mailbox.css defines the admin send form and recent send history as a two-column admin workspace on desktop and a single-column flow below 900px.
  • The mailbox styles are imported through the existing import-only domain entrypoints: src/styles/modals.css, src/styles/admin.css, and src/styles/home-terminal/top-strip.css.

Announcement UI And Mobile

  • The home announcement entry uses a lucide-react newspaper icon in the same top-right action cluster as mailbox/settings on desktop and in the compact mobile menu on phones. A red dot indicates any unread announcement or changelog; tab-level and row-level dots inside the modal use the same semantic unread cue.
  • src/styles/modals/announcement.css is an import-only player announcement entry delegating to src/styles/modals/announcement/. The parent sheet stays bounded on desktop (min-height around 520px, capped by viewport) and near-fullscreen on phones, while its plain title lockup, segmented tabs, list rows, empty/error states, detail kind pill, and detail body use the same Bright School paper border, 8px control radius, pink selected tab, and 44px touch-target vocabulary as other player windows. The announcement title no longer carries a separate pink decorative badge or dashed frame; its wrapper class avoids the lock substring so Bright School locked-state selectors cannot misclassify it, and rounded controls reserve padding/line-height so labels are not clipped by their radii. Detail headers use one fixed vertical gap so the kind-to-title and title-to-time distances match, and the detail body flexes through the remaining modal height while keeping the sheet's bottom inset visible even for short announcement bodies. src/styles/themes/bright-school/firewall/announcement-controls.css is imported after the generic surface firewall so announcement row buttons keep their 8px row-card treatment instead of inheriting broad item/card chrome. Lists own their internal scroll regions, and details open through a child .nested-modal-backdrop inside the parent announcement modal; the detail backdrop is absolutely bounded by the parent window and the nested detail modal fills that parent-sized layer on desktop and phones. src/styles/mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail/window.css is the final mobile safety override that clears inherited nested-modal full-viewport positioning, restores parent-relative same-size embedding, and beats Bright School's generic full-width nested modal rule.
  • src/styles/admin/announcements.css defines the admin management page as list + editor workspace on desktop and a single-column mobile flow. Desktop shows edit and Markdown-lite preview side by side; mobile uses an edit/preview segmented switch. Soft-delete uses a normal confirmation dialog that shows the entry title and warns there is no restore entry.
  • Announcement styles are imported through the existing import-only domain entrypoints: src/styles/modals.css, src/styles/admin.css, and src/styles/home-terminal/top-strip.css.

Story Player UI And Mobile

  • src/modals/StoryPlayerModal.jsx is the reusable player story surface for onboarding and item-character interaction scenes. src/styles/modals/onboarding-story.css remains the import-only player story modal entry split into onboarding-story/ shell, portrait/text, actions/skip-confirm, and mobile files. It defines the modal as a Bright School vertical theater sheet: a centered paper portrait area at the top with plain centered speaker-name text, no separate title/subtitle header, the largest area reserved for dialogue text, and bottom controls for next, finish, or branch options. Desktop defaults to a 4:5:1 grid ratio for portrait, text, and controls. The right-top pure-icon fast-forward button owns the skip-confirmation entry and replaces the ordinary close button. The skip confirmation is an absolute child overlay of the story modal, centered inside the parent window with width and height capped to that parent instead of inheriting generic full-screen nested-modal rules. The default text area owns its own scroll behavior so long lines do not resize the stage.
  • Story portrait options are not limited to playable/admin character rows. src/shared/storyPortraits.js merges the normal character catalog with story-only portraits before admin node selection and player-side rendering; story-only ids such as denia-rainbow-glow may appear in node characterId and render in StoryPlayerModal, while item trigger target selects stay restricted to real characters.
  • The typewriter state is visible through the dialogue text region and a small caret treatment. Clicking the text region while typing fills the current node immediately; branch option buttons normally remain hidden until the current node text is fully visible. Individual options may set revealDelaySeconds; those options appear after their own non-negative delay from node entry, and all remaining options appear when the text completes.
  • Story nodes may set effect=long-text-compress-portrait. That mode keeps the default story text region's initial size, then lets the text row grow with typed content, squeeze the portrait region down to zero if needed, and only enable text-region scrolling after the text reaches the modal's viewport-bounded height. The effect also runs the typewriter at 1.5x the default speed. Bright School's portrait mobile modal shell must keep a matching effect-specific grid-template-rows override after its default onboarding story modal grid so the themed phone layout does not revert to non-compressing rows.
  • Story options with an empty nextNodeId are intentional close-window actions. Admin publish validation allows those empty targets while still requiring option labels and rejecting non-empty targets that do not exist in the current script. Player rendering continues to route those empty targets through StoryPlayerModal close behavior.
  • Mobile uses the same story order as desktop but lets the sheet fill most of the viewport with safe-area padding and touch-sized controls. The Bright School mobile modal shell explicitly preserves the onboarding modal's grid layout instead of forcing the shared flex-column modal fallback, and the portrait area keeps symmetric padding so the portrait and name stay horizontally centered. Mobile branch buttons stack vertically at equal width; when there are many options the action area can grow and squeeze the text region, with its own internal scroll cap preventing page overflow.
  • src/styles/admin/onboarding-story.css gives the admin story script editor a two-column desktop workspace with script/trigger form editing and embedded preview side by side, then collapses to a single-column editor/preview flow below the admin mobile breakpoint. The editor card grid must keep the header, script metadata/trigger params, node list, and footer actions as separate rows, with only .admin-onboarding-node-list using the flexible minmax(0, 1fr) row and independent vertical scrollbar for long node scripts. That node scroll region must reserve a stable scrollbar gutter plus right padding, and node cards/header controls must stay shrinkable so metadata fields, delete buttons, inputs, and text do not overlap or render underneath the scrollbar or adjacent preview column on desktop or mobile.
  • Player story layering is intentionally higher than warehouse and other app-level business modals on both desktop and mobile; skip/finish/close actions therefore target the story layer first, clear every story overlay state, then reveal the underlying modal state after the story closes.
  • Bright School modal shell safeguards include .onboarding-story-modal, keeping close buttons, touch targets, and modal sizing aligned with the existing player modal vocabulary.

Mailbox UI And Mobile

  • The home mailbox entry uses a mail icon in the existing top-right action cluster on desktop and in the compact mobile menu on phones. The badge combines unread mail and claimable attachment counts from the summary API.
  • Player mailbox UI uses a two-column list/detail layout on desktop. Mobile switches to a top horizontal mail strip above the detail pane, with newer mail cards on the left and older cards to the right.
  • Empty player inboxes keep the same mailbox layout as populated inboxes: the mail strip renders a compact empty row, and the detail pane stays reserved instead of replacing the whole modal with a separate empty card.
  • Mail list rows use status color as a scan cue: pink for unread or unclaimed mail, light green for read but unclaimed mail, and gray for completed mail. The detail pane uses /assets/mailbox/mail-body-paper.png with proportional cover sizing and a light frosted content overlay so text stays readable.
  • Mailbox mobile safety lives in src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/mailbox-modal.css, imported through the final Bright School portrait guard entry after chat fixes and before character detail fixes.

性能、技术债与扩展路线

本文记录技术债优先级、后续扩展建议、实时性能和高频交互优化。新增性能优化、技术债清理、扩展路线或架构风险时优先更新本分篇。

当前结论

  • CSS 体积和全局层叠仍是前端维护风险。共享基础样式已从单体 base.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/base/ 领域文件，房间样式已从单体 room.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/room/ 领域文件，且共享棋盘子入口 src/styles/room/board.css 已继续拆成 import-only 子入口和 src/styles/room/board/ 领域文件；Startorch 对局终端皮肤已从单体 room-terminal.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/room-terminal/ 领域文件，共享弹窗样式已从单体 modals.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/modals/ 领域文件，且 src/styles/modals/replay-mode-resume.css 已继续拆成 import-only 子入口和 src/styles/modals/replay-mode-resume/ 领域文件；移动弹窗安全层已从单体 mobile-modals.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/mobile-modals/ 领域文件，商业/社交/仓库样式已从单体 commerce-settings.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/commerce/ 领域文件，且 src/styles/commerce/shop-settings.css 已继续拆成 import-only 子入口和 src/styles/commerce/shop-settings/ 领域文件，Startorch 商业/社交终端润色也已继续拆成 import-only 子入口和 src/styles/commerce/terminal-polish/ 领域文件；共享响应式层已从单体 responsive.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/responsive/ 断点文件，共享移动对局层已从单体 mobile-room.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/mobile-room/ 断点文件，共享 HUD 兼容层已从单体 hud-components.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/hud-components/ 领域文件，最终移动安全层也从单体 mobile-adaptive.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/mobile-adaptive/ 领域文件，且 Bright School 最终移动兜底覆盖已继续拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/ 领域文件，Bright School 竖屏手机最终兜底覆盖已继续拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/ 领域文件；后台管理样式也从单体 admin.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/admin/ 领域文件，共享大厅/小屋/观战列表样式也从单体 lobby.css 拆成 import-only 入口和 src/styles/lobby/ 领域文件；Bright School 基础主题、早期可读性清理、早期强制清场覆盖、高权重防串色覆盖、大厅主题、静态图廊一致性覆盖、商业覆盖、弹窗/设置/结果/房间浮层清理、动画/技能/棋盘效果、对局可读性覆盖、移动端覆盖、竖屏对局覆盖、组件修复覆盖、质量兜底层和防 HUD 串色 firewall 也分别拆成 import-only 入口与 领域文件；下一步高风险目标转为仍未领域化的其他大样式文件。
